Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the search structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 90. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 842 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on search structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 90. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 842. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the search framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 200. The resulting operator achieves bound O(942/n^4).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to search theory, we decompose the problem into 200 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 942. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
